HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001266/B001","root_001692/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_01e3db19e31a038d1fef","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:9:2\",\"root_001692\",[\"B002\"]],[\"93:9:4\",\"root_001266\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yetimin tek başına kalmışlığı, ezmeyi yalnız kaba güç değil, onu toplum içinde daha da yalnızlaştıran ve küçük düşüren bir baskı haline getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0df1d61e7fb97fb41772","text":"93:10 paired the orphan with the asker, so قهر could not remain only physical/social domination; it belongs to a broader reception ethic that also includes speech. 93:11 then made the surprise sharper: speaking of نعمة is only truthful if it does not become a form of superiority over the orphan.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_24676e05bdd2a2e507cc","text":"3. **The command also exposes delayed care as a form of failure.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ي ت م:B004` is slowness or delayed kindness toward the orphan; 93:5 postpones a gift through a future horizon, but 93:6 answers orphanhood with immediate-result `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`. **Structural cue:** purposeful futurity is set beside prompt shelter, then the orphan command. **Assigned role:** the delayed-kindness branch distinguishes legitimate temporal development from withholding care from a dependent. **Abductive move:** I infer that control over access and timing can enact domination; this extends `قَهْر` beyond overt force, and the packet does not state that extension directly. **Reading change:** the prohibition can diagnose coercive delay or neglect as well as active humiliation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001266/B001","root_001692/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_28bd0aa24fbbe67911d2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:9:2\",\"root_001692\",[\"B001\"]],[\"93:9:4\",\"root_001266\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Daha önce korunmuş olan kişinin rolü değişir: aldığı barınağı, koruyucusuz yetimin üzerinde aşağı doğru işleyen bir güce dönüştürmemelidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_28cb246d758c29755b23","text":"93:10 revealed that qahr belongs to a paired system of exclusion: 93:9 protects against force from above, while 93:10 protects against verbal driving-away. 93:11 then made the correction transmissive rather than merely restrictive, since speaking of favor after the two commands links remembered protection to present treatment of vulnerable others.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_381769482414077f9874","text":"1. **Do not repeat the lost-protector wound.** Evidence: ي ت م B001 (orphaned offspring cut off from protector), ق ه ر B001 (overpowering from above that subdues), ء و ي B001/B002 in 93:6 (shelter; pity), ر ح م B001/B002 in 93:0 (tenderness; kinship bond). Structural cue: the command about the orphan directly answers the earlier recollection of being found orphaned and sheltered. Mechanism: the packet supplies prior rescue and present prohibition; I supply the transfer that receiving shelter prohibits becoming a crushing superior. Abductive move: the remembered cut-off state is meant to regulate power from above. Reading change: the command is not generic charity; it is a rule against weaponizing superiority over one whose protective bond is broken.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4280337349c8feec68e6","text":"4. **Do not push the solitary backward.** Evidence: ي ت م B002 (isolated singleness), ق ه ر B005 (backward retreat), ض ل ل B003/B005 in 93:7 (lost thing/stray animal), ه د ي B001/B002 (guidance/course). Structural cue: after the lost one is guided forward, the orphan is not to be forced backward. Mechanism: the packet supplies backward-retreat and guidance-course branches; I supply the directional opposition. Abductive move: oppression reverses the movement of guidance by driving the vulnerable behind. Reading change: \"لا تقهر\" includes not disorienting or setting back the already isolated.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_673aa7fdf8f03ec2601b","text":"1. **Received protection must not be converted into downward force.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ي ت م:B001` is a child cut off from a protecting parent, and fixed `ق ه ر:B001` is overpowering from above that subdues and humiliates. **Structural cue:** 93:6 first presents the addressee in the same orphan condition and answers it with shelter; 93:9 repeats `ٱلْيَتِيمَ` but now changes the addressee's role from protected object to responsible actor. **Assigned role:** orphanhood marks exposed asymmetry; qahr names abuse of the now-superior position. **Abductive move:** I infer role reversal plus remembered shelter → duty to interrupt, rather than reproduce, the hierarchy of vulnerability. **Reading change:** the prohibition addresses the transmission of power across experience, not only a single forbidden act.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B002","root_001692/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_71c6018b11ede868152e","text":"2. **Do not delay kindness.** Evidence: ي ت م B004 (slowing/delay in motion or care; kindness delayed from orphan), ء خ ر B002 in 93:4 (deferring), ض ح و B001 in 93:1 (delaying to forenoon), ع ط و B002 in 93:5 (giving). Structural cue: earlier delayed goodness is reinterpreted positively, but here delay toward the orphan is dangerous. Mechanism: the packet supplies different kinds of delay; I supply the distinction that divine deferral ripens, while human delayed care toward the orphan can become harm. Abductive move: not every waiting is merciful; the vulnerable person's need changes the moral value of delay. Reading change: \"do not oppress\" includes do not let kindness arrive too late.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001266/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7be771548d7d9e5d713c","text":"3. **Do not grind/cook the vulnerable.** Evidence: ق ه ر B002 (meat seized by fire until juices run), ق ه ر B004 (hard/smooth grinding stone), ق ل ي B004 in 93:3 (frying/roasting), ر ح م B004 in 93:0 (womb ailment after birth). Structural cue: food/fire/body-pain branches appear around a command involving bodily-social vulnerability. Mechanism: the packet supplies violent material transformations; I supply the metaphor that oppression extracts, heats, or crushes the vulnerable until their inner resources run out. Abductive move: قهر's non-social branches sharpen the physical cruelty latent in domination. Reading change: the prohibition becomes visceral: do not process the orphan like matter under force.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001266/B002","root_001266/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_98c6bdd82a945f2a8801","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:9:4\",\"root_001266\",[\"B002\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Ateşin eti suyunu çıkarana dek işlemesi ve sert öğütme taşı çağrışımları, ezmeyi kırılgan kişiyi iç kaynakları tükenene dek sıkıştıran maddi bir şiddet imgesine dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001692/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9a3418988d13cfdd79a9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"93:9:2\",\"root_001692\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yetimliğe iyiliği geciktirme boyutu, ihtiyaç sahibinin erişimini sürüncemede bırakmanın da baskıya dönüşebileceğini gösterir; bu genişletme doğrudan değildir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000070/B001","root_001692/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bb825802360023128665","text":"2. **Do not compound the orphan's isolation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ي ت م:B002` is isolated singleness, while fixed `ق ه ر:B001` includes humiliation and forced subjection. **Structural cue:** 93:6 had reversed isolation by gathering into shelter (`ء و ي:B001`);
